\section*{Scope and Counts}
This handout gives the mathematician's reading of the counted declaration surface used in the machine-checked provable-security proof for HAETAE.  The source set is exactly the manifest consumed by \EC{provable-security/verify-provable-security.sh}.
The counted declaration classes extracted from that manifest are
\[
\#\mathrm{Types}=63,\quad
\#\mathrm{Ops}=678,\quad
\#\mathrm{Modules}=113,\quad
\#\mathrm{Lemmas/Axioms}=1088,\quad
\#\mathrm{Hoare/phoare/equiv\ lines}=146.
\]
The EasyCrypt code column records the exact declaration line as it appears in the source file.  For declarations whose theorem statement spans several lines, the displayed line is the source declaration line; the mathematical column gives the object, predicate, theorem schema, program module, or semantic judgment denoted by the declaration.

\section*{Global Mathematical Notation}
Let \(\mathcal M\) be the message space, \(\mathcal{PK}\) the public-key space, \(\mathcal{SK}\) the secret-key space, and \(\Sigma\) the HAETAE signature space.  A randomized HAETAE signing execution is written
\[
  (\sigma,T)\leftarrow \mathsf{Sign}^{\HROM}(sk,m;\rho),
\]
where \(\rho\) denotes signing coins, \(T\) denotes the observable transcript, and \(\HROM\) is the lazy random oracle.  An adversary \(\AdvA\) interacts with signing and hash oracles, producing a forgery \((m^\star,\sigma^\star)\).  The basic advantage is
\[
  \Adv_{\mathrm{HAETAE}}^{\mathrm{euf\mbox{-}cma}}(\AdvA)
  = \Prb[\mathsf{Verify}^{\HROM}(pk,m^\star,\sigma^\star)=\mathsf{true} \wedge m^\star\notin\QS].
\]
The EasyCrypt game hops are read as experiments \(\HGame_0,\HGame_1,\ldots\) with bad events \(\mathsf{Bad}_i\), hash-query log \(\QH\), signing-query log \(\QS\), and budget parameters \(q_H,q_S\).  The two central semantic judgments are
\[
\{P\}\;c\;\{Q\}\quad\equiv\quad \forall s.\;P(s)\Rightarrow Q([\![ c]\!](s)),
\]
\[
\mathbf{equiv}[c_1\sim c_2:P\Rightarrow Q]\quad\equiv\quad P(s_1,s_2)\Rightarrow Q([\![ c_1]\!](s_1),[\![ c_2]\!](s_2)).
\]
The ROM fundamental-lemma steps have the form
\[
\left|\Prb[\HGame_i:\mathsf{Win}]-\Prb[\HGame_{i+1}:\mathsf{Win}]\right|\le \Prb[\HGame_i:\mathsf{Bad}_i],
\]
and the sampler-freshness steps instantiate \(\Prb[\mathsf{sampler\_expand\_query}(\rho)\in\QH]\le q_H/|\mathcal C|\) for the appropriate challenge/seed domain \(\mathcal C\).
